001067:     function getToolElementById(id) {
001068:         const key = String(id || '');
001069:         if (!key) return null;
001070: 
001071:         const cached = TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.get(key);
001072:         if (cached?.isConnected) return cached;
001073: 
001074:         const element = document.getElementById(key);
001075:         if (element) TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.set(key, element);
001076:         else TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.delete(key);
001077:         return element;
001078:     }

001287:     function findStationOverviewEntry(href) {
001288:         const expected = normaliseStationOverviewHref(href);
001289:         if (!expected) return null;
001290:         return getStationOverviewEntries().find(entry => entry.href === expected) || null;
001291:     }
001292: 
001293:     function activateStationOverviewEntry(entry) {
001294:         const link = entry?.link;
001295:         if (!link?.isConnected) return false;
001296: 
001297:         if (!isIosSafariWebsite() || link.classList.contains('lightbox-open')) {
001298:             link.click();
001299:             return true;
001300:         }
001301: 
001302:         // Responsive iOS markup may expose a normal Details link without the
001303:         // desktop lightbox class. Temporarily opt that exact building link into
001304:         // MissionChief's delegated lightbox handler while preventing navigation.
001305:         link.classList.add('lightbox-open');
001306:         const preventNavigation = event => event.preventDefault();
001307:         link.addEventListener('click', preventNavigation, { capture: true, once: true });
001308:         link.click();
001309:         setTimeout(() => link.classList.remove('lightbox-open'), 0);
001310:         return true;
001311:     }
001312: 
001313:     function createManagedStationIframe(stationHref, purpose = 'station') {
001314:         const href = normaliseStationOverviewHref(stationHref);
001315:         if (!href) return null;
001316: 
001317:         document.querySelectorAll(
001318:             `iframe.mc-namer-managed-station-iframe[data-mc-purpose="${purpose}"]`
001319:         ).forEach(frame => frame.remove());
001320: 
001321:         const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
001322:         iframe.className = 'mc-namer-managed-station-iframe';
001323:         iframe.dataset.mcPurpose = purpose;
001324:         iframe.src = href;
001325:         iframe.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
001326:         iframe.tabIndex = -1;
001327:         Object.assign(iframe.style, {
001328:             position: 'fixed',
001329:             left: '-10000px',
001330:             top: '0',
001331:             width: '2px',
001332:             height: '2px',
001333:             opacity: '0.01',
001334:             pointerEvents: 'none',
001335:             border: '0'
001336:         });
001337:         document.body.appendChild(iframe);
001338:         return iframe;
001339:     }
001340: 
001341:     function removeManagedStationIframe(iframe) {
001342:         if (!iframe?.classList?.contains('mc-namer-managed-station-iframe')) {
001343:             return false;
001344:         }
001345:         try {
001346:             iframe.src = 'about:blank';
001347:         } catch (_error) {}
001348:         iframe.remove();
001349:         return true;
001350:     }
001351: 
001352:     async function openStationWorkflowIframe(entry, purpose, waitForIframe) {
001353:         if (!entry?.link?.isConnected) return null;
001354: 
001355:         if (isIosSafariWebsite() && !entry.link.classList.contains('lightbox-open')) {
001356:             const managed = createManagedStationIframe(entry.href, purpose);
001357:             return managed ? await waitForIframe(entry.href, managed) : null;
001358:         }
001359: 
001360:         activateStationOverviewEntry(entry);
001361:         const normal = await waitForIframe(entry.href, null, 24);
001362:         if (normal || !isIosSafariWebsite()) return normal;
001363: 
001364:         // Safari can render a responsive Details link while MissionChief's
001365:         // lightbox binding is unavailable. Fall back to a same-origin managed
001366:         // iframe so naming and personnel-safe station reads still work without
001367:         // navigating away from the Stations tab.
001368:         const managed = createManagedStationIframe(entry.href, purpose);
001369:         return managed ? await waitForIframe(entry.href, managed) : null;
001370:     }

003970:     function extractBuildingRecords(payload) {
003971:         if (!payload) return [];
003972:         if (Array.isArray(payload)) return payload;
003973:         if (Array.isArray(payload.buildings)) return payload.buildings;
003974:         if (Array.isArray(payload.result)) return payload.result;
003975:         if (Array.isArray(payload.data)) return payload.data;
003976: 
003977:         if (typeof payload === 'object') {
003978:             const values = Object.values(payload);
003979:             const directRecords = values.filter(value => value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value));
003980:             if (directRecords.some(value => 'latitude' in value && 'longitude' in value)) return directRecords;
003981: 
003982:             for (const value of values) {
003983:                 if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
003984:                 if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
003985:                     const nested = extractBuildingRecords(value);
003986:                     if (nested.length) return nested;
003987:                 }
003988:             }
003989:         }
003990: 
003991:         return [];

003994:     async function fetchMissionChiefReverseAddress(latitude, longitude) {
003995:         const coordinates = normalizeCoordinates(latitude, longitude);
003996:         if (!coordinates) {
003997:             return { address: '', error: 'Invalid coordinates were supplied to the reverse-address lookup.' };
003998:         }
003999: 
004000:         const query = new URLSearchParams({
004001:             latitude: String(coordinates.latitude),
004002:             longitude: String(coordinates.longitude)
004003:         });
004004: 
004005:         try {
004006:             const response = await stationFetchWithTimeout(`/reverse_address?${query.toString()}`, {
004007:                 credentials: 'same-origin',
004008:                 cache: 'no-store',
004009:                 headers: {
004010:                     'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
004011:                 }
004012:             }, 10000);
004013: 
004014:             if (!response.ok) {
004015:                 return { address: '', error: `Reverse-address lookup returned HTTP ${response.status}.` };
004016:             }
004017: 
004018:             let address = String(await response.text()).trim();
004019:             if (/^<!doctype|^<html/i.test(address)) {
004020:                 return { address: '', error: 'Reverse-address lookup returned an HTML page instead of an address.' };
004021:             }
004022: 
004023:             if ((address.startsWith('"') && address.endsWith('"')) || (address.startsWith("'") && address.endsWith("'"))) {
004024:                 try {
004025:                     address = JSON.parse(address);
004026:                 } catch (_error) {
004027:                     address = address.slice(1, -1);
004028:                 }
004029:             }
004030: 
004031:             address = String(address || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
004032:             if (!address) {
004033:                 return { address: '', error: 'Reverse-address lookup returned an empty or invalid response.' };
004034:             }
004035: 
004036:             return { address };
004037:         } catch (error) {
004038:             return { address: '', error: `Reverse-address lookup failed: ${error?.message || error}` };
004039:         }
004040:     }
004041: 
004042:     function normalizeCoordinates(latitude, longitude) {
004043:         const lat = Number(latitude);
004044:         const lng = Number(longitude);
004045: 
004046:         if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng)) return null;
004047:         if (lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lng < -180 || lng > 180) return null;
004048: 
004049:         return { latitude: lat, longitude: lng };
004050:     }
004051: 
004052:     async function stationFetchWithTimeout(url, options = {}, timeoutMs = 10000) {
004053:         const controller = new AbortController();
004054:         STATION_STATE.activeControllers.add(controller);
004055:         const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
004056: 
004057:         try {
004058:             return await fetch(url, {
004059:                 ...options,
004060:                 signal: controller.signal
004061:             });
004062:         } finally {
004063:             clearTimeout(timer);
004064:             STATION_STATE.activeControllers.delete(controller);
004065:         }
004066:     }

004068:     function extractStationArea(address) {
004069:         const input = String(address || '').trim();
004070:         const postcodeRegex = /\b(GIR\s?0AA|(?:[A-PR-UWYZ][0-9][0-9A-HJKPSTUW]?|[A-PR-UWYZ][A-HK-Y][0-9][0-9ABEHMNPRV-Y]?)\s?[0-9][ABD-HJLNP-UW-Z]{2})\b/i;
004071:         const match = input.match(postcodeRegex);
004072: 
004073:         if (!match || match.index == null) {
004074:             return { postcode: '', afterPostcode: '', area: '' };
004075:         }
004076: 
004077:         const postcode = match[1].toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
004078:         const afterPostcode = input
004079:             .slice(match.index + match[0].length)
004080:             .replace(/^[\s,;:\-]+/, '')
004081:             .trim();
004082: 
004083:         const parts = afterPostcode
004084:             .split(',')
004085:             .map(part => part.trim())
004086:             .filter(Boolean);
004087: 
004088:         const countryOnly = /^(UNITED KINGDOM|UK|SCOTLAND|ENGLAND|WALES|NORTHERN IRELAND)$/i;
004089:         while (parts.length > 1 && countryOnly.test(parts[parts.length - 1])) parts.pop();
004090: 
004091:         return {
004092:             postcode,
004093:             afterPostcode,
004094:             area: parts.at(-1) || afterPostcode
004095:         };
004096:     }

004471:     function isUnsafePersonnelTrainingRegistryKey(value) {
004472:         return !value || value === '__proto__' || value === 'constructor' || value === 'prototype';
004473:     }
004474: 
004475:     function normalisePersonnelTrainingRegistryCountMap(value) {
004476:         const source = value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) ? value : {};
004477:         const result = Object.create(null);
004478:         Object.entries(source).forEach(([rawKey, rawCount]) => {
004479:             const key = String(rawKey || '').slice(0, 150);
004480:             const count = Number(rawCount || 0);
004481:             if (isUnsafePersonnelTrainingRegistryKey(key) || !Number.isFinite(count) || count < 0) return;
004482:             result[key] = Math.floor(count);
004483:         });
004484:         return result;
004485:     }
004486: 
004487:     function normaliseImportedPersonnelTrainingRegistry(candidate) {
004488:         const source = candidate?.registry && typeof candidate.registry === 'object' ? candidate.registry : candidate;
004489:         if (!source || typeof source !== 'object' || Array.isArray(source)) {
004490:             throw new Error('The selected file does not contain a Personnel Register object.');
004491:         }
004492:         const schemaVersion = source.schemaVersion == null
004493:             ? PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION
004494:             : Number(source.schemaVersion);
004495:         if (schemaVersion !== PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
004496:             throw new Error(`Unsupported Personnel Register schema version: ${schemaVersion}.`);
004497:         }
004498:         if (!source.vehicles || typeof source.vehicles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(source.vehicles)) {
004499:             throw new Error('The selected file does not contain a valid vehicles register.');
004500:         }
004501:         const entries = Object.entries(source.vehicles);
004502:         if (entries.length > PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES) {
004503:             throw new Error(`The file contains ${entries.length} vehicles; the supported maximum is ${PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES}.`);
004504:         }
004505:         const vehicles = Object.create(null);
004506:         entries.forEach(([rawVehicleId, rawEntry]) => {
004507:             const vehicleId = String(rawVehicleId || '').slice(0, 80);
004508:             if (isUnsafePersonnelTrainingRegistryKey(vehicleId)) throw new Error('The file contains an invalid vehicle identifier.');
004509:             if (!rawEntry || typeof rawEntry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(rawEntry)) {
004510:                 throw new Error(`Vehicle ${vehicleId} does not contain a valid register entry.`);
004511:             }
004512:             const safeCount = value => {
004513:                 const count = Number(value || 0);
004514:                 return Number.isFinite(count) && count >= 0 ? Math.floor(count) : 0;
004515:             };
004516:             const safeTimestamp = value => {
004517:                 const timestamp = Number(value || 0);
004518:                 return Number.isFinite(timestamp) && timestamp >= 0 ? timestamp : 0;
004519:             };
004520:             vehicles[vehicleId] = {
004521:                 vehicleId,
004522:                 vehicleName: String(rawEntry.vehicleName || '').slice(0, 500),
004523:                 vehicleTypeId: String(rawEntry.vehicleTypeId || '').slice(0, 80),
004524:                 stationName: String(rawEntry.stationName || '').slice(0, 500),
004525:                 stationHref: String(rawEntry.stationHref || '').slice(0, 1000),
004526:                 assignedPersonnelCount: safeCount(rawEntry.assignedPersonnelCount),
004527:                 assignmentScanComplete: rawEntry.assignmentScanComplete === true,
004528:                 personnelRowsSeen: safeCount(rawEntry.personnelRowsSeen),
004529:                 trainingCounts: normalisePersonnelTrainingRegistryCountMap(rawEntry.trainingCounts),
004530:                 trainingCombinationCounts: normalisePersonnelTrainingRegistryCountMap(rawEntry.trainingCombinationCounts),
004531:                 updatedAt: safeTimestamp(rawEntry.updatedAt),
004532:                 source: String(rawEntry.source || '').slice(0, 250)
004533:             };
004534:         });
004535:         const safeRegistryTimestamp = value => {
004536:             const timestamp = Number(value || 0);
004537:             return Number.isFinite(timestamp) && timestamp >= 0 ? timestamp : 0;
004538:         };
004539:         return {
004540:             schemaVersion: PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
004541:             sourceVersion: String(source.sourceVersion || '').slice(0, 80),
004542:             updatedAt: safeRegistryTimestamp(source.updatedAt),
004543:             lastPrunedAt: safeRegistryTimestamp(source.lastPrunedAt),
004544:             vehicles
004545:         };
004546:     }

004548:     function downloadPersonnelTrainingRegistryJson(filename, payload) {
004549:         const blob = new Blob([`${JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)}\n`], { type: 'application/json;charset=utf-8' });
004550:         const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
004551:         const link = document.createElement('a');
004552:         link.href = url;
004553:         link.download = filename;
004554:         link.hidden = true;
004555:         document.body.appendChild(link);
004556:         link.click();
004557:         link.remove();
004558:         setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
004559:     }

004585:     async function importPersonnelTrainingRegistry(file) {
004586:         if (!file) return;
004587:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
004588:             window.alert('Finish or stop the active Personnel operation before importing a register.');
004589:             return;
004590:         }
004591:         if (Number(file.size || 0) > PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_IMPORT_MAX_BYTES) {
004592:             window.alert('Personnel Register import failed: the selected file is larger than 10 MB.');
004593:             return;
004594:         }
004595:         let registry;
004596:         try {
004597:             const parsed = JSON.parse(await file.text());
004598:             if (parsed?.format && parsed.format !== PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_EXPORT_FORMAT) {
004599:                 throw new Error(`Unsupported export format: ${String(parsed.format).slice(0, 120)}`);
004600:             }
004601:             registry = normaliseImportedPersonnelTrainingRegistry(parsed);
004602:         } catch (error) {
004603:             personnelLog(`Personnel Register import failed: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
004604:             window.alert(`Personnel Register import failed: ${error?.message || error}`);
004605:             return;
004606:         }
004607:         const importedCount = Object.keys(registry.vehicles).length;
004608:         if (!importedCount) {
004609:             window.alert('The selected file contains an empty Personnel Register. Nothing was imported.');
004610:             return;
004611:         }
004612:         const existingCount = getPersonnelTrainingRegistryStats().count;
004613:         if (!window.confirm(
004614:             `Import ${importedCount.toLocaleString('en-GB')} vehicle register entries?\n\n` +
004615:             `This replaces the ${existingCount.toLocaleString('en-GB')} entries currently stored in this browser.`
004616:         )) return;
004617:         try {
004618:             if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER != null) {
004619:                 clearTimeout(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER);
004620:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = null;
004621:             }
004622:             localStorage.setItem(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(registry));
004623:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE = registry;
004624:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = false;
004625:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = null;
004626:         } catch (error) {
004627:             personnelLog(`Personnel Register import could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
004628:             window.alert(`Personnel Register could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`);
004629:             return;
004630:         }
004631:         dispatchPersonnelTrainingRegistryUpdate({ imported: true, vehicleCount: importedCount, updatedAt: Date.now() });
004632:         updatePersonnelTrainingRegistryStatus();
004633:         personnelLog(`Personnel Register imported: ${importedCount} vehicle(s). Existing browser register replaced.`, 'done');
004634:         setPersonnelUiValue('status', 'Personnel register imported');
004635:         window.alert(`Personnel Register imported successfully: ${importedCount.toLocaleString('en-GB')} vehicle entries.`);
004636:     }

005390:     function prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
005391:         registry,
005392:         now = Date.now(),
005393:         maximumVehicles = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
005394:     ) {
005395:         const sourceVehicles = registry?.vehicles && typeof registry.vehicles === 'object'
005396:             ? registry.vehicles
005397:             : {};
005398:         const cutoff = now - PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_AGE_MS;
005399:         const validEntries = Object.entries(sourceVehicles)
005400:             .filter(([vehicleId, entry]) => {
005401:                 const updatedAt = Number(entry?.updatedAt || 0);
005402:                 return Boolean(
005403:                     vehicleId
005404:                     && entry
005405:                     && typeof entry === 'object'
005406:                     && Number.isFinite(updatedAt)
005407:                     && updatedAt >= cutoff
005408:                 );
005409:             })
005410:             .sort((left, right) => Number(right[1]?.updatedAt || 0) - Number(left[1]?.updatedAt || 0));
005411: 
005412:         const retainedEntries = validEntries.slice(0, Math.max(0, Number(maximumVehicles) || 0));
005413:         const vehicles = Object.fromEntries(retainedEntries);
005414:         const originalCount = Object.keys(sourceVehicles).length;
005415:         const retainedCount = retainedEntries.length;
005416: 
005417:         registry.vehicles = vehicles;
005418: 
005419:         return {
005420:             originalCount,
005421:             retainedCount,
005422:             removedCount: Math.max(0, originalCount - retainedCount)
005423:         };
005424:     }

005426:     function savePersonnelTrainingRegistry(registry) {

005429:             ? registry.vehicles

005434:             || !Number.isFinite(lastPrunedAt)
005435:             || now - lastPrunedAt >= PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS;

005452:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY,

005464:                 || error?.name === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED'

005479:                 JSON.stringify(registry)
005480:             );
005481: 
005482:             return {
005483:                 saved: true,
005484:                 pruned: Math.max(primaryPrune.removedCount, retryPrune.removedCount),
005485:                 retained: retryPrune.retainedCount,

005544:     function flushPersonnelTrainingRegistry(silent = false) {
005545:         if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER != null) {
005546:             clearTimeout(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER);
005547:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = null;
005548:         }
005549: 
005550:         if (!PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY || !PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE) {
005551:             return { saved: false, pruned: 0, retained: 0, quotaRetry: false };
005552:         }
005553: 
005554:         try {
005555:             const registrySave = savePersonnelTrainingRegistry(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE);
005556:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = false;
005557: 
005558:             const pendingEvent = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT;
005559:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = null;
005560:             dispatchPersonnelTrainingRegistryUpdate(pendingEvent);
005561:             updatePersonnelTrainingRegistryStatus();
005562: 
005563:             if (!silent) {
005564:                 personnelDebug(
005565:                     `Shared training register saved: retained=${registrySave.retained} | pruned=${registrySave.pruned}${registrySave.quotaRetry ? ' | quota retry used' : ''}`
005566:                 );
005567:             }
005568: 
005569:             return registrySave;
005570:         } catch (error) {
005571:             if (!silent) {
005572:                 personnelLog(`Shared training register could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
005573:             }
005574:             return { saved: false, pruned: 0, retained: 0, quotaRetry: false, error };
005575:         }
005576:     }

005967:     function sortPersonnelCandidatesForRule(candidates, rule) {
005968:         return candidates.slice().sort((left, right) => {
005969:             const leftReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => left.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
005970:             const rightReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => right.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
005971:             if (leftReserved !== rightReserved) return leftReserved - rightReserved;
005972: 
005973:             const extraDifference = left.trainingCodes.length - right.trainingCodes.length;
005974:             if (extraDifference) return extraDifference;
005975:             return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
005976:         });
005977:     }

006020:     function getPersonnelVehicleQueue(doc, allowedTypeIds) {
006021:         const allowed = new Set((allowedTypeIds || []).map(String));
006022:         const allowAllVehicleTypes = allowed.size === 0;
006023:         const seen = new Set();
006024:         const queue = [];
006025: 
006026:         for (const row of [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')]) {
006027:             const vehicleTypeId = getPersonnelVehicleTypeIdFromRow(row);
006028:             if (!allowAllVehicleTypes && !allowed.has(vehicleTypeId)) continue;
006029: 
006030:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];
006031:             const plainVehicleLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
006032:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
006033:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
006034:             const vehicleId = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
006035:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
006036:                 .find(Boolean) || '';
006037: 
006038:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
006039:             seen.add(vehicleId);
006040: 
006041:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
006042:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''));
006043:             queue.push({
006044:                 vehicleId,
006045:                 vehicleTypeId,
006046:                 name: cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `${TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE[vehicleTypeId] || 'Vehicle'} ${vehicleId}`,
006047:                 assignmentHref: directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`,
006048:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
006049:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
006050:             });
006051:         }
006052: 
006053:         queue.sort((left, right) => left.name.localeCompare(right.name, 'en-GB', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' }));
006054:         return queue;
006055:     }

007302:     async function personnelFetchDocument(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
007303:         const response = await personnelFetchResponse(url, {
007304:             headers: {
007305:                 'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml'
007306:             }
007307:         }, timeoutMs);
007308:

007310: 
007311:         const html = await response.text();
007312:         const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
007313:         return { response, doc, href: new URL(response.url || url, location.origin).pathname };
007314:     }

007316:     function parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row) {
007317:         const text = String(row?.textContent || '');
007318:         const matches = [...text.matchAll(/\(\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*\)/g)];
007319:         const match = matches.at(-1);
007320: 
007321:         if (!match) {
007322:             return { assignedCount: null, maxCount: null };
007323:         }
007324: 
007325:         return {
007326:             assignedCount: Number(match[1]),
007327:             maxCount: Number(match[2])
007328:         };
007329:     }

007338:     function getPersonnelAmbulanceQueue(doc) {
007339:         const seen = new Set();
007340:         const queue = [];
007341:         const rows = [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')];
007342:         let ambulanceRows = 0;
007343: 
007344:         for (const row of rows) {
007345:             const image = row.querySelector('img[vehicle_type_id]');
007346:             const vehicleTypeId = image?.getAttribute('vehicle_type_id') || '';
007347:             if (vehicleTypeId !== PERSONNEL_TARGET_VEHICLE_TYPE_ID) continue;
007348: 
007349:             ambulanceRows++;
007350: 
007351:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];
007352:             const plainVehicleLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
007353:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
007354:                 return /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(href);
007355:             });
007356:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
007357:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
007358:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
007359:             });
007360:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
007361:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
007362:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
007363:             });
007364: 
007365:             // Prefer the normal vehicle-name link because it is tied directly to the
007366:             // displayed row. Edit and personnel links are safe fallbacks.
007367:             const idCandidates = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
007368:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
007369:                 .filter(Boolean);
007370:             const vehicleId = idCandidates[0] || '';
007371: 
007372:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
007373:             seen.add(vehicleId);
007374: 
007375:             const distinctIds = [...new Set(idCandidates)];
007376:             if (distinctIds.length > 1) {
007377:                 personnelDebug(`Ambulance row contains conflicting vehicle IDs (${distinctIds.join(', ')}). Using ${vehicleId}.`);
007378:             }
007379: 
007380:             const assignmentHref = directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`;
007381:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink
007382:                 || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''))
007383:                 || null;
007384:             const name = cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `Ambulance ${vehicleId}`;
007385:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
007386: 
007387:             queue.push({
007388:                 vehicleId,
007389:                 name,
007390:                 assignmentHref,
007391:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
007392:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
007393:             });
007394:         }
007395: 
007396:         queue.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, {
007397:             numeric: true,
007398:             sensitivity: 'base'
007399:         }));
007400: 
007401:         personnelDebug(`Vehicle table scan: ${rows.length} row(s), ${ambulanceRows} Ambulance row(s), ${queue.length} usable assignment route(s).`);
007402:         queue.forEach(vehicle => {
007403:             personnelDebug(`Ambulance detected: ${vehicle.name} | ID=${vehicle.vehicleId} | table occupancy=${vehicle.assignedCount ?? '?'} / ${vehicle.maxCount ?? '?'}`);
007404:         });
007405: 
007406:         return queue;
007407:     }

008671:     async function waitForStationIframe(stationHref, preferredIframe = null, maximumAttempts = 80) {
008672:         const expectedHref = String(stationHref || '');
008673:         const expectedBuildingId = getBuildingIdFromHref(expectedHref);
008674: 
008675:         for (let i = 0; i < maximumAttempts; i++) {
008676:             if (STATE.stopped) return null;
008677: 
008678:             const candidates = [
008679:                 preferredIframe,
008680:                 ...document.querySelectorAll(
008681:                     'iframe.lightbox_iframe, iframe#lssmv4-redesign-lightbox-iframe, ' +
008682:                     '.vm--modal iframe[src*="/buildings/"], ' +
008683:                     'iframe.mc-namer-managed-station-iframe'
008684:                 )
008685:             ].filter(Boolean);
008686: 
008687:             for (const iframe of candidates.reverse()) {
008688:                 try {
008689:                     const doc = iframe.contentDocument;
008690:                     if (!doc) continue;
008691: 
008692:                     const currentPath = iframe.contentWindow?.location?.pathname || '';
008693:                     const sourcePath = iframe.getAttribute('src') || '';
008694:                     const currentBuildingId = getBuildingIdFromHref(currentPath || sourcePath);
008695: 
008696:                     const exactStationMatch = Boolean(
008697:                         expectedHref && (
008698:                             currentPath.startsWith(expectedHref) ||
008699:                             sourcePath.startsWith(expectedHref) ||
008700:                             sourcePath.includes(expectedHref)
008701:                         )
008702:                     );
008703: 
008704:                     const buildingIdMatch = Boolean(
008705:                         expectedBuildingId && currentBuildingId === expectedBuildingId
008706:                     );
008707: 
008708:                     const documentMatch = Boolean(
008709:                         expectedHref && (
008710:                             doc.querySelector(`#vehicle_table`) ||
008711:                             doc.querySelector(`a[href="${expectedHref}/edit"]`) ||
008712:                             doc.querySelector(`a[href="${expectedHref}/move"]`) ||
008713:                             doc.querySelector(`form[action="${expectedHref}"]`)
008714:                         )
008715:                     );
008716: 
008717:                     if (exactStationMatch || buildingIdMatch || documentMatch) {
008718:                         debug(`Station iframe found: ${currentPath || sourcePath || 'unknown path'}`);
008719:                         return iframe;
008720:                     }
008721:                 } catch (error) {
008722:                     debug(`Station iframe access retry: ${error?.message || error}`);
008723:                 }
008724:             }
008725: 
008726:             await sleep(250);
008727:         }
008728: 
008729:         return null;
008730:     }

017019:     function parseCreditValueFromText(text) {
017020:         const value = String(text || '')
017021:             .replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ')
017022:             .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
017023:             .trim();
017024: 
017025:         if (!value) return 0;
017026: 
017027:         const patterns = [
017028:             /(?:average\s+credits?|mission\s+value|reward|credits?)\s*:?\s*~?\s*([\d,.]+)/i,
017029:             /~?\s*([\d,.]+)\s*credits?/i
017030:         ];
017031: 
017032:         for (const pattern of patterns) {
017033:             const match = value.match(pattern);
017034: 
017035:             if (!match) continue;
017036: 
017037:             const parsed = parseInt(
017038:                 match[1].replace(/[^\d]/g, ''),
017039:                 10
017040:             );
017041: 
017042:             if (
017043:                 Number.isFinite(parsed) &&
017044:                 parsed > 0 &&
017045:                 parsed < 100000000
017046:             ) {
017047:                 return parsed;
017048:             }
017049:         }
017050: 
017051:         return 0;
017052:     }

017105:     function formatRuntime(ms) {
017106:         const totalSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
017107:         const hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
017108:         const minutes = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
017109:         const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
017110: 
017111:         return [hours, minutes, seconds]
017112:             .map(value => String(value).padStart(2, '0'))
017113:             .join(':');
017114:     }

020755:     function shouldSkipLiveRequirement(name, value) {
020756:         const cleanedName = cleanRequirementName(name);
020757:         const text = `${cleanedName} ${value}`.toLowerCase();

020764: 
020765:         // V9.0.4: Ambulances are selected from the patient badge first, not from the live mission help table.
020766:         // Mission Update still handles later patient-specific ambulance requests.
020767:         if (normaliseVehicleText(mappedName) === normaliseVehicleText('Ambulance x 01')) return true;
020768: 
020769:         // V9.2.7: these are mission stats/preconditions, not vehicles to send.
020770:         // Example: Domestic flooding (Large) has "Min. Pump Capacity = 1000".
020771:         // That is litres/min pump capacity, NOT Pump x1000.
020772:         if (/^min\.?\s*pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020773:         if (/^minimum\s*pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020774:         if (/^pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020775:         if (/^water\s*to\s*pump$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020776:         if (/water\s*damage\s*pump\s*extension/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020777:         if (/\bextension\b/i.test(cleanedName) && !/vehicle|unit|officer|car|engine|pump$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020778: 
020779:         if (/only required/.test(text)) return true;
020780:         if (/^probability of /i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020781:         if (/\bprobability\b/.test(text)) return true;
020782:         if (/personnel/.test(text)) return true;
020783:         if (/patients?/.test(text)) return true;
020784:         if (/prisoners?/.test(text)) return true;
020785:         if (/transport request/.test(text)) return true;
020786:         if (/chance/.test(text)) return true;
020787: 
020788:         return false;
020789:     }

020791:     function findMissionRequirementTable(
020792:         doc,
020793:         suppliedTables = null
020794:     ) {
020795:         if (!doc) return null;
020796: 
020797:         const tables = Array.isArray(suppliedTables)
020798:             ? suppliedTables
020799:             : Array.from(
020800:                 doc.querySelectorAll(
020801:                     'table.table, table'
020802:                 )
020803:             );
020804:         let bestTable = null;
020805:         let bestScore = 0;
020806: 
020807:         const normalise = value => {
020808:             return String(value || '')
020809:                 .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
020810:                 .trim();
020811:         };
020812: 
020813:         tables.forEach(table => {
020814:             const headings = Array.from(
020815:                 table.querySelectorAll(
020816:                     'thead th, thead td, th'
020817:                 )
020818:             ).map(cell => normalise(
020819:                 cell.textContent
020820:             ));
020821:             const headingText =
020822:                 headings.join(' ');
020823:             let score = 0;
020824: 
020825:             if (
020826:                 headings.some(heading =>
020827:                     /^Vehicle and Personnel Requirements$/i
020828:                         .test(heading)
020829:                 )
020830:             ) {
020831:                 score += 10000;
020832:             } else if (
020833:                 /Vehicle/i.test(headingText) &&
020834:                 /Personnel/i.test(headingText) &&
020835:                 /Requirements?/i.test(headingText)
020836:             ) {
020837:                 score += 8000;
020838:             }
020839: 
020840:             let actionableRows = 0;
020841: 
020842:             table.querySelectorAll(
020843:                 'tbody tr, tr'
020844:             ).forEach(row => {
020845:                 const cells = Array.from(
020846:                     row.querySelectorAll('td')
020847:                 ).map(cell => normalise(
020848:                     cell.textContent
020849:                 ));
020850: 
020851:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;
020852: 
020853:                 if (
020854:                     /^(?:Required\s+|Maximum amount of cars to tow|Cars to tow)/i
020855:                         .test(cells[0]) &&
020856:                     /\d/.test(cells[1])
020857:                 ) {
020858:                     actionableRows += 1;
020859:                 }
020860:             });
020861: 
020862:             if (actionableRows > 0) {
020863:                 score += 100 +
020864:                     actionableRows;
020865:             }
020866: 
020867:             if (score > bestScore) {
020868:                 bestScore = score;
020869:                 bestTable = table;
020870:             }
020871:         });
020872: 
020873:         return bestTable;
020874:     }

023566:     function getUnverifiedRegistryTrainingHintScore(
023567:         requirements,
023568:         registryEntry
023569:     ) {
023570:         if (!registryEntry) return 0;
023571: 
023572:         return (
023573:             Array.isArray(requirements)
023574:                 ? requirements
023575:                 : []
023576:         ).reduce((score, requirement) => {
023577:             const eligible =
023578:                 new Set(
023579:                     getTrainingRequirementEligibleTypeIds(
023580:                         requirement
023581:                     )
023582:                 );
023583: 
023584:             const entryTypeId =
023585:                 String(
023586:                     registryEntry.vehicleTypeId ||
023587:                     ''
023588:                 );
023589: 
023590:             if (
023591:                 eligible.size > 0 &&
023592:                 !eligible.has(entryTypeId)
023593:             ) {
023594:                 return score;
023595:             }
023596: 
023597:             const qualified =
023598:                 getRegistryTrainingQualifiedCount(
023599:                     requirement,
023600:                     registryEntry
023601:                 );
023602: 
023603:             const capacity =
023604:                 getTrainingRequirementVehicleCapacity(
023605:                     requirement,
023606:                     entryTypeId
023607:                 );
023608: 
023609:             return (
023610:                 score +
023611:                 Math.min(qualified, capacity) * 1000 +
023612:                 (entryTypeId === '51' ? 50 : 0)
023613:             );
023614:         }, 0);
023615:     }

023617:     function orderStrictPoliceTrainingCandidates(
023618:         candidates,
023619:         requirements,
023620:         registry
023621:     ) {
023622:         const source =
023623:             Array.isArray(candidates)
023624:                 ? candidates
023625:                 : [];
023626: 
023627:         const arrivalIndex =
023628:             new Map(
023629:                 source.map((checkbox, index) => [
023630:                     checkbox,
023631:                     index
023632:                 ])
023633:             );
023634: 
023635:         const checked =
023636:             source.filter(checkbox => checkbox.checked);
023637: 
023638:         const unchecked =
023639:             source.filter(checkbox => !checkbox.checked);
023640: 
023641:         const hinted =
023642:             unchecked
023643:                 .map(checkbox => {
023644:                     const registryMatch =
023645:                         getRegistryEntryForMissionCheckbox(
023646:                             checkbox,
023647:                             registry
023648:                         );
023649: 
023650:                     return {
023651:                         checkbox,
023652:                         score:
023653:                             getUnverifiedRegistryTrainingHintScore(
023654:                                 requirements,
023655:                                 registryMatch.entry
023656:                             )
023657:                     };
023658:                 })
023659:                 .filter(item => item.score > 0)
023660:                 .sort((left, right) => {
023661:                     if (left.score !== right.score) {
023662:                         return right.score - left.score;
023663:                     }
023664: 
023665:                     return (
023666:                         arrivalIndex.get(left.checkbox) -
023667:                         arrivalIndex.get(right.checkbox)
023668:                     );
023669:                 })
023670:                 .map(item => item.checkbox);
023671: 
023672:         const hintedSet = new Set(hinted);
023673: 
023674:         const remaining =
023675:             unchecked.filter(
023676:                 checkbox => !hintedSet.has(checkbox)
023677:             );
023678: 
023679:         return [
023680:             ...checked,
023681:             ...hinted,
023682:             ...remaining
023683:         ].filter((checkbox, index, array) => {
023684:             return array.indexOf(checkbox) === index;
023685:         });
023686:     }

024628:     function getTrainedVehicleSelectionScore(
024629:         metrics,
024630:         trainedPhase
024631:     ) {
024632:         if (!metrics?.eligible) {
024633:             return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
024634:         }
024635: 
024636:         if (
024637:             trainedPhase &&
024638:             metrics.trainedUseful <= 0
024639:         ) {
024640:             return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
024641:         }
024642: 
024643:         const trainingScore =
024644:             trainedPhase
024645:                 ? metrics.trainedUseful * 1000000
024646:                 : 0;
024647: 
024648:         const capacityScore =
024649:             metrics.capacityUseful * 100000;
024650: 
024651:         const overshootPenalty =
024652:             metrics.overshoot * 500000;
024653: 
024654:         const categoryScore =
024655:             metrics.coveredCategories * 1000;
024656: 
024657:         const psuTieBreak =
024658:             metrics.isPsu
024659:                 ? 100
024660:                 : 0;
024661: 
024662:         return (
024663:             trainingScore +
024664:             capacityScore -
024665:             overshootPenalty +
024666:             categoryScore +
024667:             psuTieBreak
024668:         );
024669:     }

032228:     async function autoHandleMissionUpdateAfterDispatch() {
032229:         const rows = readMissionUpdateRows();
032230: 
032231:         if (rows.length === 0) {
032232:             updateStatusBox("No mission update found after dispatch.");
032233:             return;
032234:         }

032239:             rows,

032241:         );
032242:

032244: 
032245:         if (assigned) {
032246:             setTimeout(() => {
032247:                 updateStatusBox("Mission update assigned. Ready to dispatch again.");
032248:                 changeDispatchBoxColor(true);
032249:             }, 1000);
032250:         }
032251:     }

032255:     function parseMissionIdFromHrefForQueueRestart(href) {
032256:         const value = String(href || '');
032257: 
032258:         const directMatch = value.match(/\/missions\/(\d+)/i);
032259:         if (directMatch) return directMatch[1];
032260: 
032261:         try {
032262:             const url = new URL(value, window.location.origin);
032263:             const missionId = url.searchParams.get('mission_id');
032264:             if (missionId) return missionId;
032265:         } catch (error) {}
032266: 
032267:         const anyMatch = value.match(/mission_id=(\d+)/i);
032268:         return anyMatch ? anyMatch[1] : '';
032269:     }
032270:

037803:         // Give MissionChief a brief moment to accept Dispatch, but do not
037804:         // re-read old red alerts here. All requirement checks happened
037805:         // before Dispatch and the click has already succeeded.

038745:     async function waitForVehicleCheckboxListStable(
038746:         timeoutMs = 6000,
038747:         stableForMs = 800,
038748:         options = {}
038749:     ) {
038750:         const startedAt =
038751:             Date.now();
038752: 
038753:         const minimumWaitMs = Number.isFinite(
038754:             options.minimumWaitMs
038755:         )
038756:             ? Math.max(0, options.minimumWaitMs)
038757:             : 0;
038758: 
038759:         const requireNonZero =
038760:             options.requireNonZero === true;
038761: 
038762:         let lastSignature = '';
038763:         let stableSince =
038764:             Date.now();
038765: 
038766:         while (
038767:             Date.now() - startedAt <
038768:             timeoutMs
038769:         ) {
038770:             const snapshot =
038771:                 getVehicleCheckboxListSignature();
038772: 
038773:             if (
038774:                 snapshot.signature !==
038775:                 lastSignature
038776:             ) {
038777:                 lastSignature =
038778:                     snapshot.signature;
038779:                 stableSince =
038780:                     Date.now();
038781:             }
038782: 
038783:             const elapsed =
038784:                 Date.now() - startedAt;
038785: 
038786:             const stableElapsed =
038787:                 Date.now() - stableSince;
038788: 
038789:             const loadControlVisible =
038790:                 isVehicleListLoadControlVisible();
038791: 
038792:             const loadingIndicatorVisible =
038793:                 isVehicleListLoadingIndicatorVisible();
038794: 
038795:             if (
038796:                 elapsed >= minimumWaitMs &&
038797:                 stableElapsed >= stableForMs &&
038798:                 !loadControlVisible &&
038799:                 !loadingIndicatorVisible &&
038800:                 (!requireNonZero || snapshot.boxes.length > 0)
038801:             ) {
038802:                 return {
038803:                     ready: true,
038804:                     count: snapshot.boxes.length,
038805:                     signature: snapshot.signature,
038806:                     elapsed
038807:                 };
038808:             }
038809: 
038810:             await wait(125);
038811:         }
038812: 
038813:         const finalSnapshot =
038814:             getVehicleCheckboxListSignature();
038815: 
038816:         return {
038817:             ready: false,
038818:             count: finalSnapshot.boxes.length,
038819:             signature: finalSnapshot.signature,
038820:             elapsed: Date.now() - startedAt,
038821:             timedOut: true
038822:         };
038823:     }

038825:     async function ensureVehicleListLoaded(options = {}) {
038826:         const stableTimeoutMs = Number.isFinite(options.stableTimeoutMs)
038827:             ? Math.max(1500, options.stableTimeoutMs)
038828:             : 10000;
038829: 
038830:         const stableForMs = Number.isFinite(options.stableForMs)
038831:             ? Math.max(400, options.stableForMs)
038832:             : 1200;
038833: 
038834:         const loadTimeoutMs = Number.isFinite(options.loadTimeoutMs)
038835:             ? Math.max(5000, options.loadTimeoutMs)
038836:             : 60000;
038837: 
038838:         const pageProgressTimeoutMs = Number.isFinite(
038839:             options.pageProgressTimeoutMs
038840:         )
038841:             ? Math.max(3000, options.pageProgressTimeoutMs)
038842:             : 15000;
038843: 
038844:         const minimumSettleMs = Number.isFinite(options.minimumSettleMs)
038845:             ? Math.max(0, options.minimumSettleMs)
038846:             : 800;
038847: 
038848:         const requireNonZero = options.requireNonZero !== false;
038849:         const missionKeyAtStart = getLocalMissionInstanceKey();
038850:         const loadingStartedAt = Date.now();
038851: 
038852:         let loadClicked = false;
038853:         let clickedPages = 0;
038854:         let lastCompletedControlToken = '';
038855:         let lastCompletedVehicleSignature = '';
038856:         let loadFailureReason = '';
038857: 
038858:         while (Date.now() - loadingStartedAt < loadTimeoutMs) {
038859:             const currentMissionKey = getLocalMissionInstanceKey();
038860: 
038861:             if (
038862:                 missionKeyAtStart &&
038863:                 currentMissionKey &&
038864:                 currentMissionKey !== missionKeyAtStart
038865:             ) {
038866:                 loadFailureReason =
038867:                     'mission changed while loading vehicles';
038868:                 break;
038869:             }
038870: 
038871:             const vehicleDisplayBar =
038872:                 getVisibleVehicleListLoadControl();
038873:
